# Chapter 3. UI/UX, readability, copy and voice, onboarding, screens, controls, mobile vs PC

Sources: 358 T3 records (2026-09-28 to 2026-11-03) plus docs/DESIGN_BIBLE.md. Each rule carries its date and source tag. Words in quotes are the director's own, unaltered. Where a record is a spec written by an agent from his request (not his words) it has no quote marks.

Precedence: a later director decision beats an earlier one. The 2026-11-03 story-first order ranks everything: (1) story generator, (2) deep simulator, (3) approachable game. UI serves the story; where a screen, control or tutorial competes with a story lever, the story lever wins (cut audit 2026-11-03: promote story levers, do not bury them).

## 3.A The pillars of interface

1. Readability and intuitive interfaces are the second pillar. "readability and intuitive interfaces are a core pillar of this project, paramount to its success. Its a challenge for an info heavy game, but its what we have to aspire for". Judge any screen, brief or critic verdict by it. [2026-10-13, memory/club-colour-and-bold-everywhere; DESIGN_BIBLE second pillar; memory note, unverified against a transcript timestamp or roadmap row]
2. Every screen: sections feel distinct, a player's allegiance is obvious, no text is overlooked. "looks good but all the text being the same colour harms readability, we need club colours and strategic use of BOLD to help sections feel distinct, players allegiance obvious, and make sure no text becomes overlooked". "this logic needs to apply to the entire game". [2026-10-13, memory/club-colour-and-bold-everywhere]
3. Applying it: each section distinguishable by weight, size or colour; a player's or club's side obvious without reading the name (contrast-safe club colour); nothing the coach needs left in uniform grey; check before tabling a screen. Club colour is never mistaken for GOOD/BAD or the primary action. Every name in running text is in club ink (UiKit.club_ink, never club_name_colour). [2026-10-13, register/CLAUDE.md; release-1-0 plan Global Constraints]
4. Club-coloured player names: "player names need colours to indicate what team they belong to, aka 'Josh Worrel is roaming the backline', 'Tom Stewart is your spare' ... tint the names in club colours". Hub names and card titles too ("Lachie Jaques is pushing for games"): "i feel like even this text should colour player names ... otherwise it just looks inconsistent". He asked repeatedly ("ive told you to build it countless times over the past few days and you keep overlooking it"): a high-priority fix is owned end to end the same turn (CI, merge, itch push, devlog). Built as NameInk (#743, #751). Adelaide's name colour is exactly FFC83A (Crows gold, #771), protected by test. [2026-10-14, roadmap/27 A1; memory; TEST_SUITE_AUDIT]
5. Club score colour reaches 4.5:1 on the scoreboard panel; UiKit.score_colour(code, surface) keeps the club's own colour wherever legible, else falls back. [2026-10-06, backlog item 20]
6. UI and readability captures never use Collingwood: "stop testing UI and readability questions with collingwood, black and white tells us nothing about how colour will display". Use "freo, melbourne, wbd, saints etc" as your club and opponent; dark-club contrast stays handled in code but is not what he is shown. Older Collingwood sets are re-taken. [2026-10-13, memory/no-collingwood-in-ui-captures]
7. Written restraint rules that were not his: "yes you may, those were never rules I said should exist to begin with, that was claude hallucinating my intent" (on "Ordinary labels are TEXT or MUTED" and "Surfaces carry no colour"). Unattributed rules in CLAUDE.md and VISUAL_STYLE_GUIDE may be reworded; only rules traced to his words bind. [2026-10-13, memory/written-rules-may-be-claudes-not-his; memory note, unverified: quote not found in any transcript or roadmap row supplied]
8. Not a spreadsheet simulator. No excessive UI; the player should not feel they will develop arthritis from clicking. One clear job per screen; if two elements answer the same question keep the better one; do not build UI because data exists. Primary screens answer "what is happening, what matters, what can I do"; detail lives in secondary screens and drill-downs; coaching, reports and matchday UI are never debug dashboards. [2026-10-09, DESIGN_BIBLE; brainstorm 2026-10-09; roadmap 1.7]
9. Readability roll-out: batch 1 Season stats, awards, trophy room, season review; batch 2 This week, Coaching, Staff, Club Forge, menu. Phase 5 sweep: one screen per PR in order of how often he sees it (hub, match view, breaks, team builder, training, draft, contracts/trades, profile, stats, menus), before/after at 390 and 1920 px, then his look; match-view centrepiece pass ("looks like PowerPoint"). [2026-10-13/18, capture_readability_b1/b2; release-1-0 plan Phase 5]
10. Accessibility: no state by colour alone [P-10]; text size setting Standard/Larger/Largest, no colour-blind palette, no whole-UI zoom [P-04]; kit clash checked under colour-blind simulation [P-34]. A tired or injured player is told apart on live view and bench strip without a tap (short mark, words on long press, no fatigue percentage) [P-06]. [2026-10-18, release-1-0 plan]
11. Review test: without the AFL names and logos, would the screen still look like this game or like an AI-generated sports-management app? If the latter, simplify; authored and restrained, not weird, never at the cost of usability. No generic AI-template design; visual references are selective (AFCM is not a visual aspiration; Footy Redraft and AFCM are negative references for the visual-style criterion only). [2026-10-06/09, register/CLAUDE.md; roadmap 24-10]
12. Visual identity: avoid dark green/charcoal rounded cards everywhere, gold as the universal accent, nested rounded rectangles, piles of pills/chips/badges, every fact in its own card, generic dashboard grids, big corner radii, excess separators, decorative stat boxes and icons; prefer flat editorial layouts. Style guide bans: raised tiles, pills, chips, badges, progress bars for anything but genuine progress, icon rows, stat boxes, cards inside cards, a tile grid as navigation, two-row tab strips, decorative dividers, any control whose only job is to look like a control. The problem is visual style (card geometry, radii, generic palette), not information density. [2026-10-06/09, register/CLAUDE.md; VISUAL_STYLE_GUIDE s3; roadmap 24-10]
13. "Not boring" (director 2026-10-08, per CLAUDE.md): club colour as atmosphere, big type and motion, 2.5D art; gradients and glows, club-tinted surfaces and turf/chalk/crowd decoration are allowed when deliberate and override the restraint rules where they conflict. Accent red only for the primary action, selection is ink with a 2 px outline (faint club tint rejected), GOOD/BAD only for genuine state. [2026-10-09/10, register/CLAUDE.md; VISUAL_AUDIT s9]
14. Typography: weight and size before colour; sentence case unless AFL convention (OVR, POT, Grand Final); no ALL CAPS labels or tiny uppercase metadata; the condensed face is for scores. "A, ARD Signwriter at every size" (Barlow pairing rejected and removed). Font changes are project-wide decisions. [2026-10-06/10, register/CLAUDE.md; VISUAL_AUDIT s9]
15. Components: panels only for real grouping, no border, no radius above UiKit.RADIUS; secondary buttons are outlines; no mini design system per screen; shared language lives in scripts/ui/UiKit.gd. Kit: primary button club fill last in column; secondary flat PANEL at most three visible; danger button BAD outline; segmented for 2 to 5 short options; tabs one row (over five means a picker); sheet with Done primary; club marker guernsey 22 px (code on chest from 32 px). [2026-10-09, register/CLAUDE.md; VISUAL_STYLE_GUIDE s3]
16. Space use (standing complaints): "stop building me UI full of wasted space and tiny text" (E5). "this looks horrible. so much space, i already told you that having all tis space between divorces the number from the source and harms readability" (A12): numbers sit beside their names (UiKit.near_figure, 160 px gap bound tested at 1280 and 2560, audited on every screen). "watch out for empty space like on the left there, no point in having that": columns balance, no tall empty column, actions together not full-width bars, nothing hidden behind a toggle when room allows. "the space between the text and the buttons feels enormous, divorcing the two sides and splitting focus": keep controls beside the line they change in a readable-width column, never edge to edge. [2026-10-10/13/14, memory/pc-is-not-a-big-phone; roadmap/27 A12, E5; the "space between the text and the buttons feels enormous" quote is a memory note, unverified]
17. "never again the oversized sparse scrolling-row list": no near-full-width rows with minimal text, ratings at the far edge, large empty areas and lost lineup context. Supporting selectors are compact, searchable and filterable by position/role/trait and allow swaps beside the visible field. A new screen must not reintroduce the pattern under another name. [2026-10-07, roadmap 02-03-18]
18. No bare numbers: every figure recognisable at a glance ("56 votes", "71 goals", count in its own right-hand column); on phone put the club on a second line under the name rather than drop the unit. Names never truncate (wrap to two lines, controls yield); stats screens are not spreadsheets (his complaint S1: eight columns at 390 px, truncated names). Money is compact AFL-scale ($1.63m, $970k), never raw values. [2026-10-05/07/10, user_cbebaec3; VISUAL_AUDIT s3.5; roadmap 19-9-1 P2]
19. Stats rows: faint band (UiKit.PANEL) every other row, no rules; right-aligned numbers under right-aligned headers; rank in its own muted column; zeros muted; rows 32 px PC / 40 px phone; short names on phone; real notation ("4.2"), no abbreviations. [2026-10-07, user_5778f315; VISUAL_STYLE_GUIDE s4.5]
20. Ceremony and decoration read as atmosphere, never as tactical advice, choices, stat changes or new rules; distinguish by placement, hierarchy and scene context, not colour alone. Fanfare comes from pacing, reveal, hierarchy and football context, not particle spam; no fake suspense (reveal stored results only); skippable. A flavour scene is judged on recognition, distinction and enjoyment; the tactical decision-clarity gate stays for tactical scenes. Banners (FL-002, approved #400) are distinct from game information. [2026-10-06/09, roadmap 21-9-3; roadmap 10-03]
21. Persistent identity labels are for meaningful actors (taggers/targets, hot players, roaming interceptors/spares, matchup actors), not all 36 players: "labels only" (no coaching-view overlay or route arrows). Consider naming the current ball carrier beside the circle, no overlaps or stale labels. [2026-10-06/07, roadmap 11-01 ARD-M8-003; roadmap 02-03-06]
22. Freckles are removed from the game: "just remove freckles from the game, unnecessary detail" (repeated: "i alreeady said to delete freckles from the game"). [2026-10-06, c03/c04]

## 3.B Copy and voice

23. Natural AFL language: what a coach, recruiter, commentator, player or supporter would actually say; no pseudo-football, engine terms, DEF/MID/FWD, enum names, raw multipliers or diagnostic labels; sentence case; Australian spelling. Labels name football things (Ladder, Players, Awards, Fixture, Trophies), never "Analytics", "Dashboard" or "Stats hub". [2026-10-09/18, register/CLAUDE.md; roadmap 1.8; VISUAL_STYLE_GUIDE s5]
23a. Auto-pick is the Team selection button's name and is exempt from the engine-word ban in item 23 (director 2026-11-12: "Auto pick. I already answered this question"; same as 2026-11-11 "Keep 'Auto-pick'"). No football rename, no "Best side". [2026-11-12]
24. Every line of copy is scrutable and tied to a real game effect: "directly recognisable as something which will impact the game". "dont ask questions about vague things and assume I can read your mind", "quit giving me vague crap". Applies to game copy, documents and playtest books: every check is one concrete question he can answer by looking, Pass/Fail spelled out, buttons use the copy's words; no "right in the hand", "does it land", "reads right", "is it still ours"; if honest copy sounds wrong, fix the mechanic; audit every visible line in one pass before handing over. [2026-10-07/10, user_6da0c968; memory/copy-must-be-scrutable; the "dont ask questions about vague things" and "quit giving me vague crap" quotes are memory notes, unverified]
25. Rejected copy, as worked examples: "what is \"he is not made the shooter\", I wish youd stop adding these vague yet declarative statements to the copy" (play-through copy states what the role gets: a forward target more scoring opportunities, a midfielder more possessions, a defender priority use of the ball out of the back half, better suited to small defenders; approved, merged #508); "the carlton one is gibberish \"they hold it by foot and mark it?\" means nothing, \"they give opponents time\" also meaningless with zero gameplay ramefications"; "playing through is also too vague, it needs to refer to his position, is he a forward target, the main midfielder? it needs to make sense immediately"; "but \"through Matthew Jefferson\" reads confusingly". A label's items list in the label's order ("says forward, mid, back, but shows a mid, fwd, back, be consistent", A7). [2026-10-07/14]
26. Player-facing text never uses dev language or working names: "what is 'papers cards'?, dont use dev language in player facing copy" (patch notes included: no papers cards, match view, bench strip, role trial, story cards, break screen). Plain wording when asking him anything: "what do you mean my look at the weather scenes". [2026-10-06/14, memory/copy-must-be-scrutable; user_c9dce4bf]
27. Decision gates: "Every decision gate says what each option does for you and what it costs, with the size and the odds, in football words. The game never says which option is better: every option carries a positive and a negative, and the weighing is the coach's. Randomness is fine when the gate has named the risk." Format ruled "B: percentages" ("their shots 25% more accurate", "misses 17% of the time"). His reasoning: "I've had a change in heart regarding my philosophy of only using football language to describe the effects of levers. I still want the football language, but I also want full transparency behind each decision and the impact it will have. How can I expect the players to make sound choices and have agency if theyre constantly rolling the dice. To be clear, RNG is still okay, as long as the risks are clearly defined in the decision gate." And: "I want to reverse this philosophy, but the decision should still be relatively opaque and both options carrying positives and negatives." An option with no downside is a design fault. Bible s4 replaced (#812). [2026-10-18, DESIGN_BIBLE principle 4; roadmap/30 rows 27-28; DECISION_TRANSPARENCY_AUDIT]
28. Off the gate, a number appears only when it helps the player understand or decide (score, rating, contract, ladder position, margin, real football rate); detail behind drill-downs. "plain football language with simple to understand maths": numbers measured in things the player never sees ("17% of what a strong base needs") are rewritten (weeks before/after, XP against a senior game's 37, plan named with fluency from/to). The 21 "no digit on screen" checks are retired ("Retire all 21"); numbers are judged per screen by style guide and screen critic. Fitness-base footer: "Rule + real effect". [2026-10-18/19, roadmap/30 rows 36, 48, 49; VISUAL_STYLE_GUIDE s4.4]
29. Decision sheet form: two lines on what happened; choice as a segmented control or at most three secondary buttons, each stating upside, downside, size and odds in plain words, never which is better; one primary action. No default recommendation cue: every decision button shares the same styling, viable choices carry equal prominence (#496, regression-enforced). [2026-10-07/18, roadmap 02-03-04; VISUAL_STYLE_GUIDE s4.4]
30. Make the rules transparent, do not make the decision: show opponent facts and player capabilities, let every rule be looked up exactly; no "best choice" labels, suggested line-ups, recommendation prompts, optimal matchup hints or "one more X" checklists on the main surface; opposition info never tells the player how to win. Changing pinned wording means changing its tests (test_story.gd:205, test_finals_cards.gd). [2026-10-05/07/18, register/CLAUDE.md; GENRE_ENJOYMENT_RESEARCH s4.4; audit s4]
31. Misleading UI is a defect (pre-bounce box showing Balanced, live-looking momentum meter, plan text quoting numbers the engine does not use, Form implying a play effect, unmentioned morale effect, hot-card duration text, understated counters). 17 story cards resolve a silent default and now announce it ("If you don't decide: [default], with its upside and downside"); every system names its one trade-off; Culture's header said "They have no effect yet" [P-63]; the effects are now built (rule 5.62, "Build the effects", 2026-10-18). Ship order approved ("Order stands"): key match-up ratings, risk-card multipliers, Who tags/Skip copy, draft leak, story cards ("Approve all 17" costs; "Yes, all six" upsides), club gates, dominant options. Shipped: #835, #818, #830, #821. [2026-09-29/10-18/11-03, SYSTEM_REALITY_AUDIT; DECISION_TRANSPARENCY_AUDIT and RULINGS]
32. Stat Guide: "Words instead": percentages and coefficients become football words ("a big edge", "a little more often", "rarely"); keep counts the player acts on (squad of 22, 6 on bench, 3 positions to be a Unicorn, "up to two traits" (now up to five, 4.2 item 22a, 2026-11-12), "8 weeks"). Scale: "the biggest part" 40%+, "a big part" ~30%, "a good part" ~20-26%, "a small part" under 20%; durability "a week to most of a season" (#398). Then: "the stat guide is like 3 pages of text. condense it, keep only the essential info": one short line per stat, grouped, about one PC screen (#757, 1816 to 362 words). Also "overwhelming long scroll of dense paragraphs": rework to a compact topic grid with search, explanations beside real UI via first-visit tutorials, claims checked against mechanics. [2026-10-06/07/14, c02/c04; roadmap 02-03-17; roadmap/27 A17]
33. FL-001 words: "Football words (Recommended)" for the Stat Guide; "Ratings" replaces Attributes; "Keep "XP" (Recommended)"; "Play the round" replaced Simulate (later "Sim the week", commit e517d1d9, now the live label); setting was "Ask before playing a round for me", now the "Ask before simming" setting (itself a cut-audit candidate, 3.Q or 1.Q7); Hub never says "match simulation"; finals tie-break never says "seed". Final pre-match opens "Finals footy. Here we go."; run-out caption "Through the banner". [2026-10-06, c06; roadmap 21-9-3]
34. Flavour and humour: FL-001 scope is optional captions and incidental details; essential action labels stay plain; no new dialogue/news framework, copied catchphrase bank, forced slang or manufactured real-player quotes; silence is valid. Humour limits, game-wide: at most one per screen; never beside a loss, an injury, a sacking or a suspension; never the same joke two weeks running. No copy beyond the two approved lines is written without his review. Banner: "200 games. Take a bow, {display_name}." Banners recognise only facts known before the match, never predict a first goal; defender headline "{display_name}: {supported_fact}". Guidance voice: real football terminology, clubroom personality, occasional humour, accurate action before flavour, never a prescribed winning strategy, matching the final UI and input device. [2026-10-06/07/09, roadmap 21-9-3; AFL_FLAVOUR_FL001_COPY_REVIEW; roadmap 02-03-17]
35. Real AFL names alone are the default ("the director's choice of 2026-10-06, \"for now\""): Settings/New career "Player names" switches to generated names; no alias prefix or "plays like"; numbered placeholders never shown; mode never changes IDs, ratings, draft logic or results; generated future drafts keep fictional names in both modes. The legal side stays his call. [2026-10-06, DESIGN.md; roadmap 09-04] SUPERSEDED IN PART 2026-11-12 by 35a: the "Player names" switch and generated names for real players are removed.
35a. **Real names only (director 2026-11-12).** "remove 'generated names' from the game, Real Names only". The "Player names" choice is gone from New career and from Settings, and every real player is always shown under his AFL name; an old settings file or save that chose generated names loads and shows real names. This supersedes the switch in 35 and in 4.7 ("Settings switches to generated names"). Invented names stay only for people who have no real name: generated future draft prospects, invented coaches, journalists, international prospects and Club Forge clubs and players. The pre-made histories ship with real names only. The public demo's rights question (TODO row 56; the "Real names and rights" rule) no longer has the fictional-names fallback: rights to real names are the only path to anything public. [2026-11-12, director message; PR "Real names only"]
36. Specific wording: Club Forge "change \"guernsey colour\" to \"main colour(s)\" and \"pattern colour(s)\" unless the plurality there makes no sense? im honestly kind of confused" (built Main/Pattern/Trim, #498). Key matchups name who is on whom ("Moore is on Curnow.", "Put Moore on Curnow", "Nobody is on Curnow."). "Assign defensive forward" / "No defensive forward assigned" (not "Nobody"); "Their loose defender" ambiguity fixed by naming player and club. "No tag: your midfielders play their own game." "Plan fluency: high / medium / low". Rating line: "One sentence for all". Player sheet speaks like "Ruck ability is below average for his position", never an invented weakness. Hub venue line "Away · Marvel Stadium · Wet" (forecast a fact, not advice); match heading "Marvel Stadium / at Essendon" order fixed. "Any out-of-contract players you leave undecided will be re-signed for two seasons if the cap allows."; off-season copy names players, contracts, offers, picks and trades, never "anything" or "done". [2026-10-05/07/13/14, various]
37. Review requests make the thing to look at obvious: "please make it more obvious what im trying to look at, so tedious having to navigate this workspace and guess what youre asking". Visual questions carry labelled images (WITHOUT/WITH, the spot boxed), a clip for anything that moves, and say what changes in the game experience; audit findings in gameplay terms. "virtually impossible for me to tell from still images": looks are judged from motion clips, not stills. [2026-10-06, backlog "The rule"; user_5fef3e70; c04]
38. Copy voice: "B: senior coach" (the senior coach's briefing). One coach voice on every screen, no signed staff lines; "we / our" for the club and its players, "you" only for what happens to the coach personally (the sack, an offer, a player's opinion of you, a promise you made). Same day: "Keep the numbers" (OVR, POT and the 0-100 opinion score stay visible as numbers, the opinion written "%d out of 100"); "Keep broadcaster" (the match feed keeps a broadcaster's voice, set apart from the coach's; "!" allowed there only); all three research topics get builds, not reports. Rules in VISUAL_STYLE_GUIDE §5.1, banned list in scripts/core/CopyRules.gd. Same day: "Sim the week" keeps the word "sim"; "simming" stays banned, so the setting reads "Ask before Sim the week". [2026-11-10, docs/research/COPY_VOICE_RESEARCH_2026-11-10.md, #1299, #1301, #1303]

## 3.C Mobile versus PC

38. Mobile first, portrait first: an Android phone in portrait is the reference design (validate ~360, 390, 412 px); no hover-only information; thumb-sized targets (44 units); deliberate scrolling; natural Android Back; no one-character-per-line wrapping or accidental horizontal scroll. Phone is portrait only (2026-11-05): "remove portrait mode... no one will play it landscape anyway" was ambiguous, so he was asked "Which one should the phone game drop?" and answered "Drop landscape". project.godot handheld orientation is portrait, the Screen setting is gone, a saved landscape or follow-the-phone choice loads as portrait; PC keeps its wide layout. Phone web browser: asked "How should the web game get it?", he answered "First tap": the first tap anywhere puts the whole page fullscreen and holds portrait (a refused or exited fullscreen is asked again on the next tap); iPhone Safari has no lock, so there it follows the phone. PC browser untouched. (Supersedes 2026-10-25 "make the afl game auto portrait mode in mobile": Screen setting Portrait, Landscape, Follow the phone.) Upright oval on phones approved (B3). Web build keeps html/experimental_virtual_keyboard=true (his clips of 2026-10-14). [2026-10-09/14/25, register/CLAUDE.md; roadmap/31 row 1; tools/check_export_data.sh]
39. PC is not a big phone: "it looks like we are using a mobile UI on a PC game". His canvas is 3840x2160 at 300% DPI (1920x1080 window = 640x360 units, fullscreen 1280x720). PC layouts use the screen in large landscape shapes (from about 760 units to 1280); no empty columns; no tap-to-reveal when there is room; actions sized to words; desktop words (click, not long-press); balanced columns; gameday style (club backdrop, one hero, your club in colour). "the oval is way too zoomed in on PC ... no reason to be so limited in our PC display". Dark theme only; light theme parked ("im also happy to abandon 'light mode' for now, i dont want to waste time on it"). He plays on PC, so desktop density, empty-space and scrolling feedback stands alongside the mobile reference. [2026-10-06/09/10/13/18, memory/pc-is-not-a-big-phone; PC_UI_AUDIT; release-1-0 plan]
40. PC scaling (STYLE-07, done #371, P0, promoted above deferred desktop work): density = max(OS DPI/96, fit to 1280x720); first window opens at OS scale; one scaling route, no per-screen font overrides; short forms centred not a tiny island; fullscreen/windowed transitions keep state. "Yes, scale it": web canvas scales on big monitors to the desktop 1280x720-at-2x look (#791). Stats screens centre mid-screen on wide displays: "centre the content mid screen rather than anchored left". [2026-10-06/07/18, roadmap 23-9-5; roadmap/30 rows 2, 11]
41. Scrolling: "i thought you knew i dont want scrolling whenever possible?" Split a scrolling screen into stages; "team selection being scrollable doesnt feel good, i think it should be 2 screens" (phone two screens, PC side by side, no scroll). Events and the news ticker come first on the hub, emphasised in club colour and bold: "the events are central to the identity of the game and not an after thought". Check every screen at 960x540 and 390x844 for a scrollbar and what sits above the fold. Preserve scroll position after every action. A press that turns into a scroll is neither tap nor long press; decorative controls never swallow swipes. [2026-10-05/13/19, memory/no-scroll-events-first; memory/pc-is-not-a-big-phone; roadmap 09-03, 19-9-1]
42. One thumb on match day: every live-play control in the lower part of the portrait screen, nothing needs a scroll while the ball is in play [P-25]; the score and clock never leave the match screen through a call, break panel, stats view or vignette, asserted by a phone-size screenshot test [P-26, C-PC-01]. [2026-10-18, release-1-0 plan; CPC]
43. Navigation economy: each weekly fact (ladder, fixture, bio, draft order, board goal, calls at the last break, cap) at most two taps from the hub, enforced by test [P-60, C-PC-02]; long-press any football word for its exact rule, one source shared with the stat guide [P-61, C-PC-04]; search any player or club from the hub, shortlist by long-press [P-65, C-PC-09]; one shared overflow check walks every screen at phone size and each text size [P-77]. "Yes" to bundle 6 "Fewer taps, nothing hidden" (P-60, P-61, P-63..P-67; P-68, P-69 skipped). "When I sim the week" standing-orders sheet: the assistant follows set lines and stops only for what no line covers [P-66, C-PC-25]. "Report a problem" is one tap and copies build, seed, round, screen, last twenty feed lines; nothing sent [P-11]. [2026-10-18, roadmap/29 Q6; CPC; release-1-0 plan]
44. Every player name opens his profile, Back returns: "virtually every time you see a players name anywhere in the game i want to be able to click on it and open their bio" (A16; #766, #768). Inline name links tap at line height; buttons and Profile taps keep 44 px ("Keep"). [2026-10-14/18, roadmap/27 A16; roadmap/30 row 7]

## 3.D Screens and controls

45. Hub: hero match poster, two or three facts, decision (Pick the side, Play match), standing (one Display figure and a line), one row of four nav buttons at the foot (Training / My list / Coaching / Sim the week), Settings in the top bar; every Hub week carries one big call tied to an arc plus the routine jobs (roadmap 32 row 6; BigCalls.gd behind weekly_checklist, off until he plays it); only a week with no call due looks quiet. The round screen leads with story cards; League news = club-colour bar, bold lead headline, 3 items, More; PC two columns. "remove it, idk why it exists" removed the "Answer it before the bounce" line and #804 option rows. League news: "awful. make it look like website frontpage rather than a ticker feed" (lead, secondary, grouped briefs, your club's item marked, no "Difficulty" line), on PC below/beside the ladder; the first full page was rejected: "no, too much empty space, stop building me UI full of wasted space and tiny text", rework to 3 balanced columns at 1920 with bigger type. "footy is too big" (#772). [2026-10-14/15/19, roadmap/27 A20, A21, E1, E5; agent-handoffs/lead; VISUAL_STYLE_GUIDE s4.1]
46. Coaching is one scrolling page (staff, "How we play", list profile, how we win/get beaten, form, cap, board confidence), plain-English insight first, numbers second; a board line on Hub only when the job is at risk. List profile: Contest, Control, Running power, Pressure, Aerial power in league-relative words (Elite/Strong/Average/Weak), no underlying score, no radar, no "recommended plan"; tap a row for what it is and who leads. "How we get beaten" shows genuine patterns only by mid-season (empty reads until 10 games); turnover lines removed (#214). "Needs a lift" must not list injured players. [2026-09-28/30, 10-05, roadmap 09-00, 09-05, 19-9-1; user_5fef3e70]
47. Settings and sim: one Settings sheet from menu and hub top-right; confirm before simming ("Simulate Round X? Your match will be simulated instead of played."; Sim round / Cancel / Don't ask again; re-enable in Options > Gameplay); match speed 1x/2x/4x/8x default 4x; Delete this career with explicit confirmation. Genuine blocking decisions may interrupt a sim. "Sim the week" is the only fast path, one round at a time, finals included. Sim to round 1 from pre-season: "Yes, on the settings in force". Vignettes On/Off: global, default On, persisted; Off bypasses playback but never auto-picks, skips the sim or hides decision info; results byte-identical. [2026-09-28/10-07/13/11-03, roadmap 04-m1, 09-04; roadmap/26 row 2; roadmap/32 row 8; ROADMAP 1.11]
48. Team selection is "ONE team viewer, the interactive field view": AFL field shape with interchange; tap a position then a player, drag on desktop and tap-tap on phone; Auto-pick is an action not a mode (the button is named Auto-pick, director 2026-11-12, 3.B item 23a; Rest tired players, Blood the youth, My Selected Best 23); manual edits stay authoritative; undo; synergy completion buttons disabled with the specific missing requirement; "N short" fact on Selection ("Build it", "Keep on Selection", no suggested swap); opposition toggle read-only, labelled projected. Desktop: field plus interchange left, compact player-card grid right (position, role, traits, name, OVR, useful POT, age, height, fitness visible without tap); phone keeps hierarchy without cramped columns. Shape is a functional selection surface, actions sit at or near the selected player, OUT-to-IN offers a few suitable replacements plus a path to the full list, never a "best". Every card shows its top three rated attributes ("EVERY player card must display that player's top THREE rated attributes"; now ranks "all 13, Durability and Star power included"). The same fact is never shown twice ("dont approve c) it displays the same information twice"). [2026-10-05/07/11-03, roadmap 08-m5, 02-03-16, 02-03-18; roadmap/32 row 18]
49. Match flow: Start goes banner then opening bounce; calls sit in the pre-bounce pane (PC three call columns, no column more than 30% empty, Game plan leads column 1, Tag and Key match-ups stay together). "More calls" is gone: all levers shown, each option (default included) with a persistent description. Key matchups are a compact grid ("Our defence"/"Their defence" with Change), not repeated sentences; the live plan summary wraps, never clipped; "One line + Change, as on the phone" for "Tag which player?". Momentum meter: both clubs' colours, persistent label, touch-accessible plain explanation, authoritative value, first-use tutorial. Feed: own goal positive, conceded negative, behinds and tactical lines neutral, never colour alone (see Open). [2026-10-07/18/11-03, roadmap 02-03-xx; roadmap/30 row 15; roadmap/32]
50. Breaks and results: full match stats at every quarter break (cumulative, same coverage as full time, reuse Stats screen, never resumes or commits) replaced the rejected halftime report ("ugly, sparse, repetitive"); shared MatchStatsView (#507); compact team comparison groups and dense sortable player tables. Full time: result first and big, what it settled (3 lines max), How it went (up to three reasons), best players as 5-4-3-2-1 votes, three team numbers; one review (Summary, Stats, Report), one way out. One concise coaching report, no duplicate scores or quarter dumps; stats worm, quarters and team stats on one PC page without scrolling (A14). [2026-10-07/14, roadmap 02-03-07, 07-m4 ARD-M4-009; roadmap/27 A14; DESIGN.md]
51. Season stats (the director's highest priority at the time, 2026-10-07, merged as #509 on 2026-10-08, so history and not a standing rank; the 2026-11-03 aim ranking, roadmap 32 row 1, does not address it): "Season stats" replaces "Full ladder" with Full ladder, Player stats, Awards race, Fixture, Trophy room. Ladder: authoritative order, Pos Club P W L D % Pts on phone, own club bold, tap a club for its read-only side; LadderScene retired (#593). Fixture opens on the current round, completed rows show both scorelines, upcoming marked by weight not colour, never invent unkept stats. Players as leaderboards (picker, one toggle, one stat at a time on phone). Looks approved; his phone review is the remaining gate. [2026-10-07/09/10, roadmap 02-03-17; user_6da0c968; roadmap 01-0]
51a. Season stats club line (director, 2026-11-06): on the Players rows the club code line under each name uses the club's strongest real colour, never white and never the ground tint. The rule: from the club's real colours only (primary, secondary, and the third only for the three-colour clubs), white, near-white and greys left out, the most saturated one that reaches 4.5:1 on the panel; if none does, the most saturated real colour lightened, hue kept, until it reaches 4.5:1. A readable red is allowed on this line. Wherever the same club-code ink is used on a Season stats list, it changes too. (2026-11-06)
51b. Trophies season table (director, 2026-11-06): for the season being played, before the finals start, the Finals cell reads "Round N of M" (e.g. "Round 3 of 24"), not "In progress"; during the finals it reads the week ("Qualifying final" etc., or as the game names them); afterwards, the result. (2026-11-06)
52. Press conference is a full-screen scene ("why is press conference still not a full screen scene?"; own Router scene, 2.5D room, journalist and outlet, answers with odds lines, result in-scene, Done/Skip/Back to hub; landscape uses ~88%, bigger type, A18). Club Forge is one deliberate main-menu destination for Create a club and Create a player; flow name club, pick design (pattern before colour), tap the jumper part to colour, "B: paint the guernsey"; Create a club approved ("looks fantastic, well done"); a live character preview throughout creation (P0); strengths excluded from weakness choices and vice versa. Main menu minimal: title, Continue only when a save exists, New Career, quiet How to Play/Settings, a clearly labelled Exit game. [2026-10-06/07/19, c02; roadmap 10-04, 10-05, 11-02]
53. Draft and lists: inspecting a player is read-only until Draft/Sign; one position row (All, then a card per position with count and need, not by colour alone); list need in one football-readable model ("Forwards: short 2"), no double counting, never names the correct position; opponent POT a narrowing range; free-agent lists sort by OVR/POT/Age reversibly; career-stage filter All/Rookies/Prime/Veterans; Training multi-select by long-press; Training row name stack centred (#277); Full Ratings readable on narrow portrait; the Ins/Outs and board-expectation briefing opens the new season ("What changed? What does the club expect now? Then begin the season.") with no second flow in the draft. Live pick trading on draft night built (#848). [2026-10-05/07/25, roadmap 08-m5, 09-03, 09-06, 04-m1; open-requirements row 46]

## 3.E Onboarding

54. "Onboarding should explain the weekly loop contextually, be skippable, and avoid a giant tutorial." "**Avoid onboarding bombardment for CLEARLY obvious UI functionality.**" Hidden or alternative interactions and important levers get an explicit first-use explanation anchored to the control: "**All alternative, hidden or essential interactions must have explicit explanations**, including **multi-select, long press**". [2026-10-07, roadmap 11-02]
55. "Tutorials: On / Off" toggle at New career, persisted through save/load, changeable in Settings; Off suppresses automatic popups but keeps Help; no mandatory tutorials; seen state persisted with reset; per-menu first-visit tutorials with football flavour and Next/Done/Skip; replayable Help. "One useful fact at a time": 1-2 sentences, never delays a critical interaction, no "Did you know?", never tells the optimal move. Tutorial copy matches the real flow and input device. [2026-10-07, roadmap 02-03-17; roadmap 10-07]
56. Onboarding follows the real implemented flow: "Walk through genuinely **fresh saves with full onboarding enabled**, not a mature save with tutorial flags already set." Cover initial draft, weekly loop, first postseason ("On a **fresh career's first postseason, once the finals are over**, introduce the postseason cadence and order before the player begins its management decisions."), early elimination, reload, skip/off/replay; "Do not mark complete from a welcome popup or copy review without checking the real flow." Unimplemented modes are recorded honestly, not invented. Trade and contract tutorials are "concise, accurate and specific — never vague"; Combine copy explains tests without prescribing a pick. Skipping help never skips football decisions. Assistant-defaults guidance lives in first-visit tutorials, not permanent paragraphs. [2026-10-07, roadmap 11-02, 02-03-11]
57. Validation: phone-sized stills and motion at narrow widths and on native Android; observed sessions check viewers tell flavour from actionable info; Brownlow viewing has Watch/Pause/Resume, Next round, Skip current round, Skip remaining count (presentation only, never votes or winners). [2026-10-06/07, roadmap 21-9-3, 10-03]

## 3.F Evidence kept as context

- Genre research 2026-10-05: AFCM (a few connected pressures, no second dashboard of modifiers), Retro Bowl (compress weekly interaction, quiet weeks quick), visual comparators (hierarchy must survive phone scaling; withholding information creates guessing). FM26's collapse came from navigation economy, the basis of P-60.
- Cut audit 2026-11-03: almost nothing is pure noise; the week holds nine match calls, each real but unfelt; story levers (backing a kid, captaincy, press answers, approaches) are buried. Plan: promote them into one weekly "big call" (BigCalls.gd, behind weekly_checklist, #859 #861 #867 #877 #881). Noisiest: Club Forge create player (cosmetic), intra-club match, football budget area, Rotations, Defensive forward/Loose interceptor, five audio rows for one job.
- Transparency audit: 113 gates, 28 HIGH, 35 misleading-copy items, 26 gates with a dominant option; of 240 story choices 54 gain-only, 55 cost-only, 11 do nothing, 120 both.
- Open defects, not rules: fatigue parity doubt, implausible generated veteran, goal-line scramble "nonsense", bye falsely ended the season, stray curved line in boundary-snap vignette, press tint flicker (A13), tag moving left called "heinous, awful UI design" (A2).

# Chapter 4. Roster, draft, trades, contracts, free agency, development, training, AI clubs, expansion

How to read this chapter. Every rule carries its date and a source tag in square brackets. Where the record has the director's words they are quoted exactly; where it has none the rule is the record's own statement of what was decided or built. Conflicts are resolved by "later director decision wins", and by the 2026-11-03 story-first order (1 story generator, 2 deep simulator, 3 approachable game), which ranks everything. Dates marked "undated" come from records that carry none. Items cut or replaced are in "Superseded and cut"; items still waiting on the director are in "Open for the director". Bible principles that bind this chapter (DESIGN_BIBLE.md): the risk belongs to the player; decisions move the odds, never the certainty; every decision gate says what each option does for you and what it costs, with size and odds, in football words; no excessive UI.

## 4.1 Lists, sides and the numbers a club carries

1. A side is 18 on the ground plus 5 interchange, 23 in all, no substitute role. Director P0, 2026-10-06: "Implement ARD-M5-001 in `docs/ROADMAP.md`: **18 players on ground + 5 interchange, 23 total, no substitute role**." Five bench must work through manual and AI selection, rotations, injury cover, participation, stats, XP, appearance tracking; old saves preserved. [2026-10-06, user_5778f315_c03; roadmap 24-10]
2. The All-Australian team is 23 players: "AA team should also be 23 players". [2026-10-06, user_5778f315_c03]
3. Dual ruck: "5th interchange spot will be filled by the second ruck if the player elects to run dual ruck" (Ratings.DUAL_RUCK_MARGIN 5.0, One ruck / Dual ruck choice in auto-pick). A user-nominated backup ruck is a later requirement under ARD-M5-005; rotations, injuries and bench swaps must never silently assign a nonsense ruck. [2026-10-06 / 2026-09-28, user_5778f315_c03; 04-m1 M1-002]
4. Ruck integrity: genuine ruck contests when a genuine ruck exists, emergency ruck only with no genuine option and at a sensible disadvantage; hit-outs credit the actual contestant (hit-outs to non-ruckmen fell 51.5% to 6.1%, PR #60). [2026-09-28, 04-m1]
5. Extra Midfielder rucks (director's reference examples: Luke Jackson, Brodie Grundy, Tristan Xerri) have stronger contested grunt around stoppages and less aerial potency than tap-focused rucks, through real events, never fabricated credits or forced traits. Ruck draft principle: Gawn goes in round 1 only to a "now" club; best ruck under 30 by pick 36; a "good ruck" is under 33 (RUCK_FIRST_CHOICE 65, SECOND_STAR_RUCK 0.6). [2026-10-05 / 2026-10-07, 07-m4; user_5778f315_c07]
6. List size: the target is min(44, floor(pool/clubs)), currently 37; MAX_LIST 44; only two rucks mandatory; rookie numbers start from 41. Guernsey numbers are unique within a club roster (opposing clubs may share); duplicates repair deterministically without changing identities. [undated DESIGN.md; 2026-10-07, roadmap 02-03-06]
7. Real names are the default. Generated names come from a large plausible contemporary Australian pool, never fantasy words or numbered placeholders; international prospects' names must fit their origin. [2026-10-05 PR #268; ARD-M8-009 #149; 2026-10-14 roadmap/27 C1] SUPERSEDED IN PART 2026-11-12 by 3.35a ("Real Names only"): real names are the only names for real players; the generated pool now names only people with no real name.
8. Role labels must match the player. Small defender only at 186 cm or under; midfielders with 3.5+ clearances or 8+ contested a game are never Wing; 24 listed positions filled; zero sim effect; named fixes (Miers a forward, Uwland not key, Langford a wing) are pinned by tests/test_roles.gd. "why do we call it rebounding defender? i keep getting confused between interceptor and rebounding, call it Small Defender to be consistent in our naming". [2026-10-14, ROLE_AUDIT; 2026-10-07 #497]
9. Money is real-AFL-scale: 2027 cap $18.44m, senior floor about $155k, salaries to $1m+, pick-band first-year contracts. "Real AFL money means simulated club salaries and caps, not real-money purchases. The game has zero microtransactions." Match payments, ASAs and club profit/loss are out of scope. [2026-10-05, roadmap 09-03; #198/#208]
10. Wet-weather trait: "Evidence + stats". Whitfield (GWS) and Dangerfield (GEE) by name; everyone else earns it by the stat rule (contested 78 and disposal 72 or more, mids and ruck only, 6.3% of the 2026 list). Weather: "do your own research into how weather impacts footy and draw your own conclusions" (wet favours contested footy, dry eases handling and marking). [2026-10-06, user_5778f315_c04/c05]
11. Crossover match rules recorded here: concussion means at least two matches out, no manual early return, same for AI (CONCUSSION_MIN 2, UI "Concussion — X matches"); tag targets are midfielders only; "any player can intercept*" but the loose defender gets more if good at it; the career baseline is the completed 2026 season, careers start 2027. [2026-09-28, 04-m1; 2026-10-07 PR #510; 2026-10-06 user_5778f315_c04]
12. Named-player rating buffs are one-off and are not licence to alter the generator: Rozee attributes x1.10, cap 99 (OVR 53 to 55, POT 79); Bowey "buff boweys ovr 5% and his pot 5% too" (OVR 68 to 71 by smallest uniform attribute factor, POT 72 to 76, once, only him); Langford attributes x1.15 (OVR 57 to 64, POT 77 to 82). Rating balance stays the director's call. These are director-ordered one-off exceptions (rule 5.165 lists the Rozee and Bowey buffs as director-added), recorded here as the explicit exception to the ban on named-player special cases (rules 4.39 and 8.11): nobody else gets one, and spot-checks of famous names are calibration evidence, not buffs. [2026-10-05/06, c01/c02; PRs #213, #267]

## 4.2 Ratings, potential and development

13. POT is the projected natural peak, not a hard cap and not raised because OVR overtakes it. "What he can still reach": from age 26 POT = reachable_peak(p), easing past 30. "Tighten them": POT ranges tightened (your POT reads window half+1; intake base_pot_half 3/4). Human and AI develop under the same rules, no difficulty-based hidden XP (rule 4.63 allows only the visible difficulty setting to move rival development, trade margins and your XP, never match strength; read the two together, 4.63 being the earlier 2026-09-29 audit text); training is dearer near or beyond POT; rare breakout rolls become career stories. [2026-10-01 PR #156; 2026-10-07 user_5778f315_c07]
14. Draftees: "Start lower, climb at the real rate ... but some players are more ready-made than others" (PR #695, needs the director's own playtest; balance is never declared done by an agent). [2026-10-13, roadmap/26#7; 2026-10-15 AUDIT]
15. Director request: "slow the kids down to match real AFL" (growth-curve audit, set against real lists). [2026-10-13, growth_curve_impl]
16. Rollover order: intakes merged, age +1, development bands, retire oldest and lowest (floor 32 a club), generate next class, undrafted carry over and age out at 22. [undated DESIGN.md]
17. Training XP after every game: a full senior game 37 XP, reserves half share 19 XP, injured or suspended only 4. Plans are kinds of footballer: Position plan (default) and archetypes (Inside midfielder, Wing, Key defender, Small defender, Key forward, Small forward, Ruck). Results say what training changed (OVR rises, traits unlocked, potential reached), not stat points bought. The 2026-11-03 cut removed Manual and the old Star power plan. [2026-11-03, DESIGN.md#Training; roadmap/32]
18. Training must not turn a physically unsuitable player into a key-position archetype (label fixed to "Training as a key defender"). [2026-10-05, roadmap 19-9-1 P0]
19. Retraining: "retraining adds another playable position to the player's toolkit". It never removes prior positions or degrades their attributes, grants no elite attributes, and the new position still needs the learning process (verify sequential retraining and save/reload). [2026-10-07, roadmap 02-03-16]
20. Learning a position is a bounded development project (RC-003 accepted "within existing owners"): 8 fit weeks; passes if rating there is within 3; one project per player per season, two at a time per club; size gates key forward 192 cm+, small forward up to 181, key defender 191+, ruck 196+; POT 70+ for a second position, POT 90+ for a third ("Rarer Unicorns instead"); price: own-position training limited to +2 that season, payback next season +1 never past POT. Plausible move only (rating there within 6). No guarantee of POT, extra currency or separate engine. [2026-10-05/06, roadmap 08-m5 ARD-M5-003; GENRE#7]
21. "projects must have an impact": auto-pick plays a player at a learned position whenever he rates higher there than the line's weakest starter; a learned third position counts wherever positions matter. Merit ranking fills lines only and keeps BENCH_COVER, the dual-ruck choice and the ruck slot. "Yes, sparingly (Recommended)": AI clubs learn positions, each running one project a season on its highest-POT candidate, no news items. [2026-10-06, roadmap 08-m5; user_5fef3e70_c02/c04]
22. The Unicorn (a player pickable at forward, midfield and back) fills one missing place in one synergy, never two ("director's wildcard rule; rarity is the control"). [2026-10-06, roadmap/18 duplicate-merge-map; 08-m5]
22a. **Traits: up to five, plus Unicorn (2026-11-12).** "i would like players to earn at maximum 5 traits + unicorn if they achieve it"; "i want MORE traits around the league in general, i dont want to hamstring a player's ability to gain traits. Traits make the teambuilding way more fun and recruitment more engaging and feed into the RPG feel". A player earns every good trait whose line he clears, up to five; past five he keeps the five he clears by the most; Unicorn (and the wet-weather player and Hothead) come on top. The old two-trait limit, where a third showed only once he was further past its line than past one he had, was never his ruling and is gone, with every line explaining it: the near-trait line reads "Engine at 91 durability: 4 to go." Every club the same. [2026-11-12, director to the parent session]
23. Synergies are build specialisations, not completion bonuses: harder to activate, stronger, a real roster commitment and opportunity cost, a balanced list should not collect them all. "synergies must have an impact"; "synergies should be harder to achieve."; "Widen player spread (not lower thresholds)" so generated players reach the trait thresholds (disposal 86, marking 76, intercept 80, durability 91, creating 75, accuracy 71). Targets: each synergy on for about 15-25% of clubs after a season, clubs mostly 0-1, one synergy worth about +10-12% win. Rules stay transparent: show "N short" (for example "1 crumber short") with no suggested swap and no best-choice label. Engine room 4 bulls, Tall-small 2+2, Intercept wall 2 interceptors + 1 lockdown, Supply line 3+1, Running machine x0.50 fatigue. [2026-10-05/06, roadmap 19-9-1; backlog items 11, 15] **Conflict, 2026-11-10:** the director: "if anything i wanna boost synergy numbers but thats for later". Superseded the same day by his target: "i think teams at the bottom of the ladder should have 0-1 synergies, teams in the top 4 probably have 2-4 synergies". The 15-25%-per-synergy and mostly-0-1 targets above no longer apply. Parked in TODO section F, unassigned.
24. Draft classes carry real list proportions: "Match real lists" (the role bag, meaning the role distribution of draft classes and lists, must match real AFL lists: about DEF 34%, FWD 30%, MID 28%, RUCK 7%). [2026-10-06, backlog item 19; #465]
25. Teaching and coaching identity: no new coach levelling tree or XP economy; staff foundations stay. (The season-ceiling point below is superseded: "Teaching is XP scaling, not the season-ceiling point", director 2026-11-04, rule 4.65.) RPG-006 slice 1 (#563, 2026-10-09): a Strong (82) or better teaching staff adds a point to a player's season ceiling, Fair (62) or worse (or a vacant job) takes one off, same rule for every club; man-management unchanged; tactics not changed yet. [2026-10-06/09, 22-9-4 RPG-006]
26. Workload carries between weeks (Fresh / Carrying a load / Needs a break shown in selection; auto-pick weighs readiness; reset at off-season; ratings and injury odds preserved): "Explicitly assigned by the director: implement idea 4, carrying workload between weeks so selection and resting a veteran before finals have consequences." [undated, roadmap 08-m5 ARD-M5-015; PR #154]
27. Passive reserves development: omitted players develop at a reduced rate; no playable reserves competition, fixture, tactics or selection screen; senior AFL stays the best development environment. [undated, 08-m5 ARD-M5-006] Superseded in part by 61d (director 2026-11-12): players left out now play real state league matches.
28. Development design lessons (research, adopted as constraints): development should change whom the coach can use and when; keep present strength versus a youngster's opportunity a real decision; no guaranteed potential fulfilment, no extra staffing admin, no hidden AI resource advantage; a club identity should require sacrifice; success is an explainable football approach, not a checklist of bonuses; no trait thresholds from other games, shop randomness, reward economies or monetisation. [2026-10-05, GENRE#3.4, 3.7, 3.8]
29. Ambition's patient side gets a small effect: "Yes, small". Draftees and under-22s at a patient club settle and develop a touch faster (about 3% / 5% by grade, final sizes in playtest); veterans' re-sign asks are not lowered; one plain line on the Culture screen. [2026-10-18, roadmap/30#8]

## 4.3 Pre-season

30. The pre-season is eight weeks, planned in blocks that stop at events: "Plan in blocks, stop at events ... but reduce it to just 1 pre season match". A new career also gets it: "Yes, the same eight weeks". Plan forgetting is slow: "Slowly" (every plan slips one step over the off-season; an unplayed plan fades slowly in the year). Time trial: "Keep both" (pre-season stop and mid-season card). [2026-10-13, roadmap/26#2]
31. There is one pre-season match at the end of week 7 (cut shipped 2026-11-03, live with no switch): it keeps the stood-out / struggled reads, side picked on Selection, prospect looks, XP and injuries; empty trial spots fill from the recruiters' top picks (PracticeFill button, automatic on sim). Practice-match rules the director approved ("Approve the seven"): opponent from regional draw, your club at home, reserves get a game's worth of development at half the injury rate, a second sighting overwrites the first note, plan-familiarity hook. It trials 6 prospects per club (data/preseason.json practice_guests). [2026-10-18 / 2026-11-03, roadmap/30#26, roadmap/32]
32. Prospects to invite show OVR, POT and projected draft ranking (recruiters' read, sorted by rank): "these prospects should show their ovr and pot and projected draft ranking". [2026-10-19, roadmap/30#50]
33. Light block: "give it a real gain" / "Give it a real gain": Light weeks heal the injured a week faster (light_heal_extra 1; the source's wording, size his call; "twice as fast" is this Bible's arithmetic reading). Drill buttons "medium, high by Round 1"; fitness footer: "Running by Round 1: N weeks. Under 4.5 is thin, 6 or more strong." (thin tires 4% faster, 6% more soft-tissue, peak 85%; strong 2% slower, 4% fewer, peak held longer). Heavy block injury risk is 0.8% per player-week. [2026-10-19/20, roadmap/30#47 for "give it a real gain" and the extra week of healing only; the drill-button and fitness-footer strings are from register/scripts/sim/PreSeason.gd:436 and PR #843 (lead-built, not director words); the thin/strong percentages and 85% peak are this Bible's own records]

## 4.4 The draft

34. Two main drafts exist, plus the mid-season rookie draft of rule 4.44 (draft calendar: League Draft opens a career; National Draft closes each season; mid-season rookie draft after round 11). The League Draft opens a career: 18 clubs share a snake order, one pool, one salary cap; the pool includes the real 2026 class (725 players; the opening lists are stated three ways, see 4.Q26). The National Draft closes each season: father-son and NGA first, then an open pool over ceil(pool/18) snake rounds (max 4) in reversed-ladder order, ending when the pool runs dry, cap a formality. [2026-09-28 M1-010; undated and 2026-11-03 DESIGN.md]
35. Salary-cap guard: usable room is cap room minus the minimum salary needed to fill remaining mandatory spots; a selection that leaves too little is illegal ("This selection would leave insufficient cap space to complete your list."); a rival with no legal pick passes; no cap breach is ever permitted. [2026-09-28, 04-m1 M1-009; PR #68]
36. Prospects are an uncertainty the player can invest in: Recruiting department (scouting uncertainty x1.25/1.00/0.80/0.65 inside a zero-sum $12m split across four departments; levels Minimal $1.5m, Standard $3m, Strong $4.5m, Elite $6m). "Prospects, and I can invest". [2026-10-04/05, user_491246e9_c01; memory/rpg-feel-vision]
37. The player never gets free exact reads: the League Draft board shows scouted estimates (#222, your recruiters' read via Draft.user_view, USER_EVAL_SD 3.0), projected prospects show a scouted range (#313), other clubs' players show a POT range that narrows with time in league and recruiting budget and is exact once on your list. Hidden-rating prospects show the scout grade word, never a number ("the scout grade word"). [2026-10-05/06, roadmap 19-9-1; roadmap/32#20]
38. Rival scouting error: "Decision (director, 2026-10-06): keep 1–5." (Draft.AI_EVAL_SD 1-5; 0-4 is tooling only); "Drop it, keep 1–5" on #228. [2026-10-05/06, DRAFT_EDGE_EVIDENCE; c02]
39. AI draft valuation is horizon-based: "Yes, and look further ahead" (HORIZON_YEARS 8, HORIZON_REPLACEMENT 58, HORIZON_NOW_SHARE 0.25; "now" versus "build" clubs; #494). "no named-player special cases": age and remaining useful seasons materially affect value, only clubs chasing a flag in 1-2 seasons treat veterans as premium early picks, building clubs favour development; no hidden AI boosts. Valuation sanity (ARD-M5-012, #148): fix the model, never individual ratings; need multiplier floor 0.2; elite falls need a legible football reason; AI obeys the same cap and list rules. [2026-10-07, user_5778f315_c07; roadmap 02-03-18; 08-m5]
40. The sim is extremely competent (director, 2026-11-03, Row 37 later ruling): every club, the user's included, acts in its own best interest at all times; mistakes come only from imperfect information, never deliberate dumbing down. The buffed draft logic is the only National Draft logic: rivals, "Pick for me", sim-through picks and live pick trades; the ai_draft_buff switch is gone. [2026-11-03, roadmap/32 Row 37]
41. Draft night: Pass / Pick for me buttons approved (#732). "implement live trading of picks on draft night" (2026-10-25): trade picks any time with no clock ("Any time, no clock"); "Everyone (Recommended)": you offer any time, rivals offer you as your pick nears, rivals deal among themselves and the board shows it; "Picks, this and next 2 drafts (Recommended)" (players stay out, lists closed; the trade period uses the same three drafts); "Same values as the trade period (Recommended)" with no night premium. Offers: AIs bid up to their own full value and only within a tenth of fair value (NIGHT_OFFER_FLOOR 0.9, NIGHT_DEAL_RATE 0.06) after "it LOOKS fine, but the trades being offered are absurd?" (pick 45 + a 2029 second for pick 6 became pick 9 + a 2029 third). [2026-10-14/25, roadmap/27 B5; roadmap/31; PR #848]
42. Late 11-03 rulings on rival draft swaps: contenders discount 2-3 year-out picks (far-pick discount 0.8) so swaps happen, overriding the 2026-10-14 hold; NIGHT_RIVAL_MARGIN stays 1% ("director keeps 1% rival margin"); 3-pick night packages. [2026-11-03, handoffs/scratch-2026-11-02; ai-club-decisions]
43. Draft pool controls after the 2026-11-03 cut: position, sort and search remain; club, career stage, style, trait, available-only and Combine sort are gone; Combine results stay on the prospect's sheet. [2026-11-03, roadmap/32 CUT_AUDIT row 9] The Combine screen keeps its ranked test tabs (Sprint, 2 km, Jump, Kick) inside the Combine; the draft pool stays without a test sort. [2026-11-10, row 83]
44. All appearance choices are cosmetic "except dominant foot". [2026-10-05/07, 24-10; roadmap 10-04]

[2026-10-18, DECISION_TRANSPARENCY_AUDIT#10B, #10C]

## 4.5 Trades

51. Trades run at real AFL volume: "increase trades" / "increase trades as you say" (about 33 trades, 13 moving a player in 2024, every club in at least one; about 21 player moves a year). Merged #383: players 20-29 can ask to be traded (go home, or for a game), rivals trade more, requests show on the trade tab ("looks good"); the market is deterministic (paired runs identical) and uses home_state from data/player_origin_2026.csv. [2026-10-06, backlog items 7, 14, 18; roadmap 09-03; c05]
52. Trade value: potential is age-adjusted (TradeValue.future_rating, growth projected by the development rule and 60% counted, "Rule, 60%"); contract value on market scale (about $120k a point, +-12% cap); a star in his rehab year is valued at the rating he comes back at; unproven potential is discounted by senior games (#224; whether to keep or soften the discount is the director's call); a learned third position adds no trade value until #228 settles. [2026-10-04/06, roadmap 19-9-1; TRADE_E_HANDOFF; COMPETITIVE_BALANCE]
53. Market design: picks and future picks tradable, no two-asset cap, persistent pick ownership, a numerical trade value per asset or package (a compact decision aid), star premium so junk bundles cannot buy an elite young cornerstone, quality-aware positional need, club phases (rebuilding / building / contending) from public evidence. Pick horizon: the current National Draft plus the following two (2027, 2028, 2029 in a 2027 trade period). Regression case: Adelaide accepting Jordan Sweet + Ryan Lester for Arki Butler (72/92) must be rejected; "leave it" on the old-star-for-best-kid trade (a real, discoverable trade). [2026-10-06 / 2026-10-10, roadmap 09-03; VISUAL_AUDIT#9]
54. Rival behaviour (director's calls): "rivals buy stars too" (a contending rival may buy anyone on a rebuilder's list at the off-season market; a club does one deal at most, league at most three); "up to 15" (GameState.TARGET_TRIES). Trade UI built around selected packages (You give / You get) with confirmation before commit. [2026-10-04, COMPETITIVE_BALANCE#11; roadmap 09-03]
55. Contenders discount far picks and rivals work to the same value rules as you. Trade difficulty may change closing difficulty but never make an irrational deal acceptable. [2026-11-03 / 2026-10, roadmap 09-03]

## 4.6 Contracts, free agency and retirement

56. Contract negotiation has ceremony: tapping an expiring player's term opens a negotiation sheet with salary and term, asking salary an anchor, bounded seeded randomness only ("no hidden dice casino"); release or delist needs explicit confirmation stating consequences; a lightweight accept-asking-price shortcut; important decisions never resolve from one accidental tap. [undated, roadmap 09-03]
57. Free agents use the same negotiation flow as re-signings (no instant "Sign 1/2/3 yr" shop buttons, no player guaranteed to join, cap impact shown first). Rival free-agency order has no first refusal by CLUB_ORDER: deterministic, only facts every club has (ladder, list, cap), no special treatment for the human, no auction (#172). Compensation picks driven by the accepted contract, projected consequence shown before signing, same rules for AI. [undated, roadmap 09-03]
58. Early extension requests only from a star: "rarer, stars only" (80 or more, one player a season; 1.8 asks a season became 0.7). "stars test the market": one of your best 23 (18 on the ground plus 5 interchange; written "best 22" in the earlier record) left unsigned at season end tests free agency (standing offer is asking price over wanted term, rivals bid, he picks on money, security, role, how the club finished); others re-sign for two seasons if cap allows; at the list minimum nobody leaves. [2026-10-04, COMPETITIVE_BALANCE#9]
59. Assistants: a light contract layer, 2-3 season terms, 1-2 expire a season, one-tap Re-sign / Let go; AI clubs follow the same rules; no sideways poaching; avoid six tedious negotiations. [2026-10-05, #225; roadmap 19-9-1]
60. Retirement persuasion: "Yes, same rules (Recommended)" for AI; "Once a career (Recommended)"; eligibility "In best 22 and at/above position bar (Recommended)", now best 23 after 18+5 (about 7 a season league-wide, one for your club about every other season). A yes is one more season, then he goes. Refusal reasons: still injured (named), three injuries in two seasons, unhappy (morale under 40), out of the side (under 8 games this year); injuries go on a player's record (injury_log) from this career on. Retirements are decided when the off-season opens; a retiring player is never re-signed, released or put on the market; a "Retiring" section tops the Contracts tab ("Ask him to go around again"). [2026-10-05/06, user_5778f315_c03; 22-9-4]
61. Neglect costs: management must matter. Measured: a passive club stays under the cap but its list fades; working the list (contracts, free agency, trades) keeps a club up, trades the biggest lever. Features must visibly matter: "why do we have these features if they dont have an impact, fix them". [2026-10-04/06, COMPETITIVE_BALANCE#3, #7; c02]
61a. Medical retirement check for concussion: "if a player gets 5 or more concussions within a 5 year period, they should have to pass a medical retirement check". The check is "Flat odds each time" (one stated chance of being cleared, the same at every check); the panel sits "Straight after the fifth" (the week of the concussion that makes five, and after each later one while the count holds; a fail ends his career that week). Every club. The cleared chance is his to tune. (2026-11-11; TODO row 154)
61b. Concussion medical retirements stay rare (director 2026-11-11, asked after 30 measured seasons gave no panel at all): "its quite rare IRL too, maybe 0.25 per year or less". Target: league-wide medical retirements at or under 0.25 a season (about one every four seasons), and above zero, measured over many seasons. Auto-pick keeps its name (director, same day: "Keep 'Auto-pick'"). (TODO row 154)
61e. Approved and merged (director 2026-11-12): the free-agency odds and the Recruiting miss line on other clubs' players and free agents (PR #1397, T1.R26 L3 rival reads), and the player-sheet concussion line with the count and the panel's odds (PR #1402, row 154).

61c. State league results simulate very fast (director 2026-11-11: "just make sure the state league results are simulated very fast"): every state league match except the coach's own uses a quick result model, not the full match engine; a whole state league round adds under 5% to Sim the week, measured. When the coach is coaching in the state league, it flips (director 2026-11-11: "if you are currently coaching in the VFL, the league simulation operates like normal. Contrarily, the AFLs simulation would then become minor background"): the state league plays like the AFL does now, and every AFL match uses the quick result model and sits in the background. (TODO row 128)
61d. The state league's shape (director 2026-11-12, row 128 interview). Interview answers (director 2026-11-12): real names for the state leagues' own players, sourced like the AFL lists ("Real names"); the first slice has the VFL, SANFL and WAFL ("VFL, SANFL and WAFL"); an AFL club's players left out of its senior side play that week's state league match for its reserves or aligned side, with their own stats, form and injuries, and development comes from those games ("They play real VFL games"; supersedes rule 27's "no playable reserves competition"); a career can start at any state league club, AFL reserves sides included ("Every VFL club"). (TODO row 128)
## 4.7 AI clubs

62. Director said yes to all three AI audit fix streams (2026-11-02): ai-match-tactics (own-club tactics, counter plan, styles +-25%, defender move) and ai-club-decisions (delists, coach dice, sack cap removed, trade caps to 40 guard, draft-night gate, projects, conditioning, FA, contract length). Competent-AI rule (2026-11-03, PR #888): coach decisions without dice, met-goal renewal; 102 senior coaching changes in 30 seasons (was 167). [2026-11-02/03, handoffs/scratch-2026-11-02]
63. AI clubs obey the same cap, list, contract and development rules as the user; no hidden AI resource advantage; difficulty changes rival development, trade margins and your XP only, never match strength (as a visible setting; rule 4.13, later, bans any hidden difficulty-based XP); keep the fantasy league draft: "A fantasy-style league draft isn't AFL-realistic, and that's a good gamification. Keep it." The fix is information (forming side against the league in words), not mechanics. [2026-09-29, DESIGN_AUDIT#1.1, 1.3, 4.6]
64. Coaching staff: six jobs per club (senior coach, senior assistant, midfield & ruck, forwards, defence, development); you are senior coach, five NPCs; skills shown only as grades (Elite 86+, Strong 78+, Good 68+, Fair); the seed is the Round 1 2026 world (103 real coaches, 5 generated, 35 outside a job); no real 2027 appointment claimed; coach aliases use the coach's own first name. The market runs once a season at close: coaches develop and retire (64-70), AI senior coaches judged against the board goal (two failed seasons is a sack), vacancies filled senior first, promotion only after two seasons, at most two of your assistants poached a year, vacancies show a shortlist of four (Appoint or Auto-fill), custom and expansion clubs start vacant and hire all six before their first season. [undated, DESIGN.md; 2026-10-07 #490]
65. Coaching effects are three small capped modifiers: Teaching scales match XP (cap -5% to +10%), Tactics scales plan execution +-15% and AI reaction, Man-management spares morale loss when left out (up to 40% at elite; never makes anyone unhappier). Boundary with rule 4.25 settled (4.Q1, director 2026-11-04): "Teaching is XP scaling, not the season-ceiling point"; this XP model governs and rule 4.25's ceiling point is superseded. [undated, DESIGN.md]
66. Genre lessons held as constraints: success must be an explainable football approach; reject RPG ability furniture and hidden AI advantages; preserve upsets; never let a good decision guarantee victory. [2026-10-05, GENRE#3.7]
