# Decisions needed from the director

Every open question from chapters 1 to 9 is collected here once. Each carries its source references in square brackets as chapter:number (for example [4:11] is chapter 4, question 11; chapter 5 questions are Q1 to Q25 and chapter 7 questions are O1 to O26, written here by number). Where several chapters ask the same thing the question appears once with all its references. The chapter text keeps the full wording and evidence; this section is the working list for the director. Answers settle the matter and the chapters are then corrected.

### D1. Scope, identity and what the game will not have

1. ANSWERED 2026-11-12 ("All but 14 and 15"): the list is in the design bible's "What this game will not have" section; P-74's page draws on it. What will this game deliberately not have? The list of deliberate absences is unset and blocks the scope freeze; P-74's list is the director's to write. [1:1, 9:1]
2. Does a "Not in this game" page need entries beyond "Playable control" and "Microtransactions" (multiplayer, 3D, spoken commentary, light mode and AFLW are not picked), and does the director write the rest? [7:8]
3. Does the bible's "Current focus" section (agency fine-tuning, visual polish, "players in the visual sim really react to your coaching moves") belong in the bible, or does it move out? It was placed on an assumption. [1:3, 5:21, 9:3]
4. Is polish goal 1 (player agency fine-tuning in matchups and rotation changes, "easily measurable") still open as written under the story-first order? Where do polish goals live, and do morale, board and press relationships exist in the sim? [1:9, 9:36]
5. ANSWERED 2026-11-12 ("Add story, reorder"): the design bible's pillars now follow the 2026-11-03 order: story, a deep simulator, then approachable play with agency. Which wording on the game's ranking stands: ROADMAP 0.2 "game first, simulation second", the project description "equal importance", or the bible's agency pillar? (Note the 2026-11-03 order: story generator, deep simulator, approachable game.) [9:10]
6. Is the README and DESIGN.md "auto-battler" framing still right, given the bible's active participation? [9:9]
7. Which stands: ROADMAP 0.1 standing authority to pick up ready items and merge, or CLAUDE.md "a roadmap entry is not authorisation"? [9:8]
8. Playtest gate 1.12 "Can I feel my decisions?" (proposed by the audit): adopt it, with pass defined as naming a decision that changed a visible contest over three matches, telling each match's story in three sentences including a player battle, and explaining a loss as personnel, opposition, decision or luck? [9:28]
9. Which of the audit's ten biggest gaps goes first (coaching not visibly reacted to, match view identity, CareerSave.VERSION mismatch silently dropping a career, onboarding, pre-season behind a switch, clone figures, others)? Which goes first: the execution queue (ROADMAP 0.4.1 Season stats patch) or the visual sim reacting to coaching (ARD-M8-003 with M4-001/002/004)? How tightly is the visual sim tied to what the match sim did? [9:6, 9:7, 9:30]
10. Which of P-01 to P-11 (beyond the 1.0 plan place), P-16, P-19, P-21, P-22 and P-24 does the director select? [9:31]
11. May the "Sim the week" and ladder wording, and the pre-timeskip, press-dice and balance sign-off items recorded unassigned after the bible audit, be assigned? Which items in the director-question batch remain (#309 player form, #449, others)? [9:38, 9:39]
12. Is the "Sim to finals" button (ARD-M1-007) gone with the "Play to Grand Final" cut, and should it be removed from the register? [7:17]

### D2. Release 1.0 and 1.1, platforms, price, licensing

13. Platforms for 1.0 (plan Q2): which ship, web on itch (live), Windows on itch, an Android APK on itch or Google Play? Play needs a $25 fee and a signing key, which is the director's written yes. The plan recommends all three on itch and Play Store as 1.1. [7:1]
14. What does "synchronised" mean: feature and version parity only, or saves carried between devices (cloud sync, export/import file)? The plan recommends parity plus a save export/import file with no server and no spend; cloud sync is 1.x. [7:2, 9:25]
15. Real names and price (plan Q4 and Q7): does 1.0 stay free with real names (fictional names as the fallback), or free, pay-what-you-want or paid? The plan recommends free or pay-what-you-want and a separate decision after a rights check before any paid release. [7:3] (2026-11-12: the fictional-names fallback no longer exists, rule 3.35a.)
16. Real-AFL licensing (AFL and AFLPA names and clubs) is unresolved: who clears it before any public or paid release, the director or a session? Do the real B&F medal names (Doig, Sutton, Barker, Truscott) ship named or generic? Is fictive-name safety acceptable? [5:19, 7:25, 9:34]
17. Scope freeze (plan Q1): does 1.0 ship the listed rows and nothing new, with AFLW, personality types (D2), the Club Forge plan builder (R41), live-3D vignettes (R38), cloud save sync, Play Store, Coach of the Year, club museum, legends wall, own game plan (R40) and the long-save wishlist moved to 1.x? [7:4]
18. What is in 1.1? ("then we do 1.1 to tune and polish it".) Which rows are 1.1: the director's looks on pre-season, draft night and the oval ring, plus which off switches and Tier A items? [7:5]

- **Tasmania at the start (2026-11-09).** Asked "With real lists and no Canberra, should picking Tasmania play 2027 without you and start you in 2028?", the director answered "Yes, start 2028". Overrules "Tasmania cannot be chosen at the start".
- **A feature that never occurs is a bug (2026-11-09).** "if a gameplay feature we add NEVER occurs in game that isnt 'rarity', its a BUG"; "dont treat 0% as 'rare', its not 'rare', its nonexistent".
- **Sir Doug Nicholls guernsey designs cut (2026-11-09).** Design library, catalogue, import and export and the artist question are cut. The annual round and its saved record stay. Overrules the earlier guernsey rulings.
- **Event cards (2026-11-09).** Rare cards: "raise their weight". Irish and NZ contract cards: rated bar "make it 75". Captain's slump and succession: rewrite for real names.
- **Itch only when he says (2026-11-09).** "we dont need to push to itch after every day or every update / just whenever I say / and the build needs to climb sequentially". No upload, version bump or devlog without his word. Each build he calls gets the next number, with its own new devlog post; a post is never retitled to cover a later build. Overrules any earlier rule to refresh itch unprompted or edit the last post for a follow-up build.
- **Legacy mode: the seasons after the start (2026-11-10).** Asked what happens in the real seasons after a 2010 start: "drafts will be era accurate but the draft picks will be subject to the simulation, creating an alternative history". Each year's real draft class enters the league; who picks whom is the sim's.
- **Legacy mode: era rules (2026-11-10).** Asked whether the rules of the era apply: "yes, they will". Each rule (free agency, academies, expansion concessions) arrives in the year it began.
- **Legacy mode: real history (2026-11-10).** "All matches will obviously be unique to the sim, but we will still have real historical events like expansion clubs joining, the hawks dees merger game, the essendon drug scandal, brown paper bags, collingwood racism scandal, etc". The span he wants: "i want people to be able to play from 1990-2100 and beyond".
- **Legacy mode: ratings per era (2026-11-10).** Shown three options after research (OOTP and Basketball GM rate against the player's own league), he chose "A: per era": every player is rated against his own season's league, as 2026 is now, and how each era played lives in the match engine. Overrules no earlier ruling.
- **Legacy mode: the start dates (2026-11-11).** Start dates in the end (director 2026-11-11: "in the end id like players to be able to pick the following start dates: 1990, 2000, 2010, 2020, current season, 2028 (tassie), 2030 (canberra)"): seven starts. The four past ones run on that era's real lists; the current season is today's start; 2028 is the Tasmania start that already exists (2027 plays without you, row 129); 2030 starts with Canberra entering, its enter year already set in clubs.csv, on the same pattern. 1990 is built first. (TODO row 142)
- **Legacy mode: into the game (2026-11-11).** "once you gather all the data, start putting it into the game": the build starts as each decade's data lands, without waiting for a look at the build order. (TODO row 142)
- **Legacy mode: the Fitzroy merger (2026-11-11).** "it always happens unless you are regularly competing in finals, and even then, the story beat still happens (with a slim chance of the merge going ahead no matter what) / if youre struggling though, no choice, the merge happens whether you want it to or not". (TODO row 142)
- **Legacy mode: the Bears when Fitzroy survive (2026-11-13).** Asked "If Fitzroy survive, what happens to the Bears?": "Bears carry on", then "bears coaches should be given the choice to rebrand to lions or stay as bears". The Bears keep their name, colours and ground and the league runs 17 clubs from 1997; if you coach the Bears, you choose: become the Brisbane Lions or stay the Bears. The look only, no effect on play. Clashing guernseys (same day): "if the lions/bears and fitzroy guernseys clash you can slightly change the colours of each (still same look, just slightly reshaded to emphasise differences)". (TODO row 142, L8)
- **Legacy mode: era football numbers (2026-11-13).** "Numbers pass" on the era year screen (#1514; 1990 frees 22.8 against the real 24.8 accepted). Balance stays open until his own playtest of a past season. (TODO row 142, L5)
- **Legacy mode: controversies (2026-11-11).** Real penalties land as in history: "real penalties land, though all these things must be togglable in the game creation screen (something simple like: \"Controversies (Drug scandal, salary cap rorts etc.): on or off\"". Events about personal conduct stay at club level: "Club level only" (never naming the individuals). (TODO row 142)
- **Legacy mode: coach bans (2026-11-13).** Asked whether a real coach ban (Essendon 2013, Melbourne 2013) lands on your coach, who replaces the club's real coach, he chose "No, club only": fines, lost picks and the finals ban land on the club; your coach is never banned. (TODO row 142)
- **Legacy mode: the supplements saga's own switch (2026-11-13).** "maybe put the drugs saga as its own toggle in game creation as its punishment (full 1 year ban of list) is far more severe": a New career switch "Supplements saga", apart from Controversies; on, the 2013 penalties and the real 2016 season bans land (docs/research/supplements_bans_2016.md). (TODO row 142)
- **Legacy mode: the supplements saga, default and top-ups (2026-11-13).** Asked whether the Supplements saga switch starts on like Controversies, he chose "Follows Controversies": a new career starts it at whatever Controversies is set to. Asked whether Essendon's 2016 top-up players are signed for you, he chose "You pick them": coaching Essendon in 2016, you sign the up-to-ten top-ups yourself from the mid-season pool on a pick screen; a rival Essendon signs its own. (TODO row 142)
- **Legacy mode: era look (2026-11-11).** Era grounds (Waverley Park, Victoria Park and the like) and era guernseys come "In the first slice" of 1990. (TODO row 142)
- **Legacy mode: guernseys per decade (2026-11-11).** "all guernseys need a 90s and 2000s and 2010s version too": every club gets a 1990s, 2000s and 2010s guernsey, worn in its decade. (TODO row 142)
- **Club Forge colours (2026-11-11).** "also please add more colours to the Club Forge". (TODO row 156)
- **Coach of the Year (2026-11-13).** "add a coach of the year award (note it is awarded for the home and away season only, finals results arent considered, and the award is given out after the conclusion of the home and away season immediately)"; "its not always teh coach whose team finishes first (though often, it is). if a team goes from bottom four to top four, chances are their coach will win the award"; "or if a team goes on a long winning streak to make finals, their coach will win the award most of the time". Supersedes two parts of item 160: finals results never count (no finals depth, no premiership weight), and the model case is bottom four last year to top four on this year's home-and-away ladder. Item 160 otherwise stands: every senior coach eligible, rivals included; the winner with one football line, no score breakdown. Given in the news the week the home-and-away season ends, before a final is played; drawn by odds, never certain (scripts/sim/CoachOfYear.gd). Shown with the reason under each award, on the coach's profile and your record: "add reasoning why he was the best coach of the year"; that look "Approved" the same day.
- **Legacy mode: watch era averages (2026-11-11).** "if the playing data for historical decades is significantly different in its averages and is subsequently overrating/underrating the players, let me know, keep an eye on it" / "because the game of AFL has changed dramatically in those eras so the stats might be different". Each decade's ratings are compared with the 2026 shape and any over- or under-rating is reported to him with the numbers. His fallback (same day): "if the balance changes considerably from era to era we will have to create a clever inflation immune/era-scaling player rating". (TODO row 142)
- **Legacy mode: ground names by year (2026-11-11).** "note that the name of 'Marvel' stadium has changed regularly in the past 30 years, it must carry its IRL era-appropriate name / a short news story in the feed can commemorate when the names change". Applied to every ground that was renamed, not only Docklands. (TODO row 142)
- **Legacy mode: potential is informed (2026-11-11).** Asked how much a real player's potential should know of his real career (young players on the 1990 lists and every real draft class after), he chose "Informed": his real peak sets his ceiling, give or take luck, so the real stars of each class tend to become the sim's stars. (TODO row 142)
- **Legacy mode: the plan interview (2026-11-13).** Asked for the legacy plan ("make a /plan for the implementation of the legacy mode including an interview with me"), he answered: your coach "You replace him" (the club's real 1990 senior coach), and the man replaced "Joins the coach pool", hireable by any club; the start is a "Year step in setup" at the start of New career; "Era rules in engine": the 1990 slice plays 1990's football, tuned to real 1990 averages; "Real 1990 fixture", and the real fixture for "Every real season" through 2026 (the generator after, or when the sim's clubs differ from history); the 1990 second tier is "VFL reserves", each club's own reserves side; real players' retirements: "Sim decides"; stats not recorded before the 2000s: "Estimate the gaps" from the recorded ones, reported with the era-averages check; after the 1990 slice, "Carry 1990 to 2026" before the 2000, 2010 and 2020 starts; the real year events: "Yes, 1990-91 ones" in the 1990 slice. (TODO row 142)
- **The real 2026 fixture for every 2026 career (2026-11-13).** Asked "Should an ordinary (non-Legacy) 2026 career also play the real 2026 fixture?", he answered "Real for 2026 too": every new 2026 career plays the real 2026 fixture, all 25 rounds with the Opening Round (the generator when the clubs differ from history, as for every real year). (TODO row 142, L6)
- **Legacy mode: a start is a campaign, not a year (2026-11-13).** "im concerned that sessions are seeming to interpret 1990 start as a standalone '1990' year task, its not, its simply the start of a 36+ year campaign"; and on the guernseys, "we need 1990-2026". Every year-dependent part of Legacy mode (rules, money, fixtures, second tier, drafts and expansion lists, era play, grounds, guernseys, year events) covers every season from the start to 2026 and follows the season being played, never the start year. "one task per session please." (TODO row 142)
- **Custom game plan (2026-11-12).** The game-plan traits are removed: "the 'game plan traits' are also not at all what I wanted when I prompted you. What you've implemented is a second gameplan, essentially, that stacks on top of the first one. Thats not what I asked for: I asked for a custom gameplan feature, where the player can create their own unique gameplan that impacts real stats in the matches. By picking from a list of unique positives and negatives from a list. I want you to remove the current implementation and reconfigure it around what I actually asked for." It is a plan of its own, chosen like any other plan, and neutral: "the game plan must not be able to directly counter another gameplan though. it must be more neutral like 'balanced' or more aptly, 'win contest'". It never counters another plan and no plan counters it. Asked how many picks, he chose "1 strength, 1 cost": the coach picks one strength and one cost from a list, each moving real match stats. Every club can run one (rival clubs build their own), and no pairing is better on the scoreboard. Supersedes the open "own game plan" questions (Q18, R40, 2.55). (TODO rows 158 and Custom plan)
- **Sim speed (2026-11-12).** Asked how to win back sim time, he chose "Speed up the engine"; on drift, "Inside AFL bands": an engine speed-up may shift stats only while every stat stays IN or NEAR the real AFL bands on `tools/balance.sh match 1000` paired. (TODO row 159; 8.Q20)
- **Weather can change at a break (2026-11-12).** "Yes, at a break": weather may change at most once a match, announced at a break, and the forecast states the odds. Overrules the earlier "Not yet" (P-36). (TODO row 160; 2.Q1)
- **Feed colours (2026-11-12).** He answered "not sure, you choose"; his 2026-11-10 ruling "your club's colour, not blue" (D6 74) stands; the question was asked again in error. Build: our goals in our club's colour (his 2026-11-10 ruling, not blue), goals against red, everything else grey. (TODO row 161; 3.Q8)
- **Pre-match calls stay pre-bounce (2026-11-12).** "Stay pre-bounce": the calls stay in the pre-bounce pane, not ahead of Start. (3.Q10)
- **A vignette on every decision card (2026-11-12).** "Yes, every card": every decision card gets a vignette. (TODO row 162; 2.Q12)
- **Autopilot is too harsh (2026-11-12).** "Too harsh": soften it so a club on autopilot drifts to mid-table, not last. (TODO row 163; 8.Q3, 9.Q17)
- **PR #1401 look passes (2026-11-12).** "Passes, merge it": copy voice pass 5, team builder and coaching (TODO row 134).
- **Teaching (2026-11-12).** Already ruled 2026-11-04: "Teaching is XP scaling, not the season-ceiling point"; the stale open question 4.Q1 is closed.
- **The draft needs list room (2026-11-12).** "Must make room": if your list is full, the draft does not start until you delist enough players for three picks or trade the picks away. Rival clubs delist their weakest the same way. (TODO row 164)
- **Press line reworded (2026-11-12).** "Yes, reword it": the design bible's press bullet now reads "Every answer carries a named risk: the odds of each outcome are shown before you answer." (1.Q6)
- **Pillars restated (2026-11-12).** "Add story, reorder": the design bible's pillars follow the 2026-11-03 ranking (story first, a deep simulator second, approachable play with agency third; readability stays its own pillar), and the feature test opens with "Does it add to the coach's story or the club's legacy?". (1.Q10, 1.Q11)
- **Won't-have list (2026-11-12).** "Yes, draft it", then, shown the 19-line draft, "All but 14 and 15": the design bible gains "What this game will not have" with the other 17 lines, each with his source words. Left out: no family trees or bred talent (14) and no web of player relationships to manage (15), so neither is ruled out. (1.Q1, D1 1)
- **Legacy money in dollars of the time (2026-11-12).** "Dollars of the time": in Legacy mode salaries, the cap and every money figure show in that season's dollars, not converted to today's. On the 1990-2012 salary cap figures reported missing: "huh? look harder"; the real figures are to be found, not left as gaps. (TODO row 142)
- **Economy interview (2026-11-13).** Asked "research and brainstorming session me for how to overhaul the economy/budget side of the game", he answered an interview (docs/brainstorms/2026-11-13-economy.md) and approved these entries ("Add all five"); the build is split into rows ("Split into rows", TODO rows 192-195).
  1. **Economy scope.** "Football money only": the salary cap, contracts and the football department. No club bank, revenue, profit or debt (the 2026-10-05 ruling stands).
  2. **Club wealth.** "Real differences", and it "Moves with success": each club starts from its real wealth, which grows or shrinks slowly with results. The AFL helps a club in need with "picks and money" by a stated rule, rivals alike.
  3. **Football department.** "Real soft cap + tax": one soft cap for all; wealth decides how far a club can spend; overspend pays the luxury tax. Staff wages come out of it ("Yes, from dept money").
  4. **Salary cap.** "Pinch plus real tools": good lists cost more, with front- or back-loading, the 95% floor, carry-over room and breach risk ("Front/back-loading, 95% spend floor, Carry-over room, Breach risk"). Answers open question 9.18.
  5. **Money touchpoints.** "Off-season screen, Pitch to the board, Press and board talk". A refused pitch costs board confidence; a granted one raises the goal ("Both").
- **Overnight merges (2026-11-12).** For the night of 2026-11-12 he allowed held-look PRs to merge after the agent's own frame check ("Merge after your check"), and PR #1453 (play-through picks) to merge if its rerun balance screen has nothing OUT of range. One itch build once all of them have merged ("Yes, push one build").
- **Merge queue on (2026-11-12).** "Yes, turn it on": GitHub's merge queue is turned on for main. Rule 1.48's sync-by-merging and no-rebase rules still apply.
- **Repo moved to an organisation (2026-11-12).** GitHub only offers a merge queue on a public repo owned by an organisation, so asked whether to move it he said "Yes, move it". He created the free organisation aussierulesdynasties; the repo is now aussierulesdynasties/Afl-auto-battler (old URLs redirect) and the "main merge queue" ruleset squash-merges up to 5 PRs at a time with "test" required.
- **Every draftee shows who he plays like (2026-11-12).** "add to every draftee in every draft one line \"Plays like: <insertnameofrealaflplayerwhosestatspreadatmaxpotentialwouldresemblethatofthisdraftee>\"", "that way if people go far into the future they can still visualise how a player will perform even if theyre a fictional player". "this is not just for flavour, it has to be based on facts"; "not just the rating spread but also the position and height"; and, asked whether only the shape of the ratings should count, "level must be included". So the name comes from three facts: the same position, about his level (a real player whose peak overall is within 5 of his POT when there is one), a similar height (within 4 cm when one of those is; height always counts) and the closest ratings at max potential (`scripts/sim/PlaysLike.gd`). It sits beside rule 35 without changing it: that rule keeps "plays like" out of a player's display NAME; this is a separate line under the name, on the National, League, Rookie and mid-season draft screens. It is the same for every club and changes nothing in the sim.
- **Visual overhaul interview (2026-11-12).** After the agent looked at 614 captures of every screen, he answered a long interview. Order: "Match day" first; player faces and hair "After match day". Review: "One look sheet per area" (the agent builds an area, checks every frame, shows one before-and-after sheet; he names faults). Art: "Rig and Tripo, no asking" (people from our rig, never text-prompt poses; props and rooms from Tripo inside the 15,000 budget; credits reported).
  - **Match day (TODO row 166).** One stadium: "Replay stadium everywhere" (live oval, call scenes, set shots, replays). Call scenes: tighter camera (grass at most a third of the frame), full sides, umpires and crowd, the set shot drawn at its real distance and angle with the man on the mark in front of the kicker, waiting figures that move (copied from real clips). Pre-match: "One screen, tap to change" (each call on a row with its current pick, one Start). Live oval: "Bigger discs, bench drawer" (about 1.5x, jumper numbers). Weather: wind shown without grass squiggles (flags, scoreboard arrow, ball drift), hot days look hot, night games added. PC: "Oval left, panels right". Bugs fixed without review: scoreboard "Q1 0'" before the bounce and "Q4 30'" at full time, ghost Skip buttons under break sheets, "Profile" over the PC full-time stats.
  - **Discs in a pack (2026-11-12, asked with phone frames of the 1.5x oval).** At 1.5x a side's discs were more than 28% hidden in 7% of frames (0.2% before) and sixteen measured spacing settings fixed either that or Attack Corridor's shape, never both. He chose "Shrink in packs": 1.5x with readable numbers in open play; a disc draws smaller only while it overlaps others. The crowding bars in tests/test_match_motion.gd stay where they were.
  - **Event cards and press (row 167).** Cards: Gain and Cost as chips (icon and number, sentence on tap), a picture per event, finals and Grand Final cards look big. Press: odds as split bars, the asking journalist pinned on screen with their own look, the rules paragraph behind a tap, the result shown with a picture and a moving meter.
  - **Hub and frame (row 168).** Hub: "Checklist as big rows" under the match card, no dead gap. Stripes: "Stripes at the top only", plain dark page behind lists and body text. Accent: "Always club colour" for the main button, with a readable version for dark or pale clubs. Rows: no row truncates or wraps a name; every player row has a club rail, bold name, grey detail. Text: "Body text too small", "Grey text too faint" (raise both; the faces stay). Motion: numbers count up, screens slide, results reveal, the hub feels alive.
  - **Money, draft, new career (row 169).** Money: "Meters and deal cards" (cap as a tappable bar; each contract or trade a two-sided card). Draft: the list gets the screen (at least 6 rows; the blurb shows once), prospect sheet with figure and bars, a better draft stage. New career: club picker as a grid of 18 jumpers, setup as steps, the first interview in the press room, the coach reveal as a card.
  - **Season end, pre-season, ceremonies (row 170).** Awards as trophy cards, the finals bracket drawn, a bigger premiership moment, the trophy cabinet as shelves. Pre-season: "One plan board plus pictures". Ceremonies (draft, Brownlow, premiership): "Rebuild to farewell standard" with crowd, lighting and movement from real clips.
  - **PC (row 171).** "Proper PC layouts": every sheet gets a wide layout, no stretched art, nothing phone-width on a wide screen.
  - **Trades check.** "Decline the letter in the test": the check's coach never answered the rival board's letter, so three of six clubs never opened the trade period; with it declined the check reads 2.00 a year. A test fault, not the market.
  - **His 1.0.15 reports (2026-11-12, asked with a quarter-time sheet of three heading inks).** "titles of sections tend to blend with the body text and nothing stands out": headings take sand, e8cf9c ("Sand (B)"). Defensive Press washing his forward 50: "Rename High Press" (the plan hunts high; 2.54's name reversed, same effects). A Forge club in Real 2026 lists: "Yes, as a new club", with the new-club package (widens 71). Tasmania in a 2027 redraft: "Join in 2028".
